### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を保証する」通信、UDPは「速さを優先し、確認はしない」通信です。

TCPは届いたかを確認して再送し、順番も揃えて届けます。UDPは送りっぱなしなので、遅れや欠けは受け手側で扱うことになります。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner al día tu rama local con la principal antes de compartirla, y así el historial queda lineal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir commits que otros ya tienen causa problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠、有序但有连接开销，UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、延迟低。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when a branch is yours and private, so you can tidy its history before anyone else builds on it. Merge when the branch is shared or already pushed, because rewriting public history breaks everyone else's copy.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径就行，直接把文件名跟在后面：

```bash
bun test tests/math.test.ts
```

Bun 会把参数当作路径过滤，写一小段名字也能匹配，比如 `bun test math` 会跑所有路径里带 `math` 的测试文件。

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/math.test.ts -t "adds two numbers"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, so that if it crashes mid-write, it can replay or roll back those changes on restart and keep its data consistent. Because the log is append-only and written before the actual data files, it also makes writes fast and durable.
```

不做的事：

- 不改 review 里没提到的其他行为。

    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记一笔后这里显示总花销</div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">按天</h2>
  <div id="days"></div>

  <details class="k-add" open>
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="d">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="d" placeholder="11-23"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="w">花了什么</label><input class="k-input" id="w" placeholder="午饭、打车、酒店…"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="a">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="a" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
var items = [];
var picked = null;
var lastDay = '';

function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
    return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];
  });
}
function fmt(n) { return '¥' + n.toFixed(2); }

function save() {
  ducter.set('items', items);
}

function render() {
  var tot = document.getElementById('total');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var days = d … tener('click', function () {
  var d = document.getElementById('d').value.trim() || lastDay;
  var w = document.getElementById('w').value.trim();
  var a = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a').value);
  if (!d || !w || !(a > 0)) return;
  lastDay = d;
  items.push({ id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), day: d, what: w, amount: a });
  document.getElementById('w').value = '';
  document.getElementById('a').value = '';
  document.getElementById('d').value = d;
  save();
  render();
});

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
  var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
    items = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== id; });
    picked = null;
    save();
    render();
    return;
  }
  var row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-id]');
  if (row) {
    var id2 = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    picked = picked === id2 ? null : id2;
    render();
  }
});

function load(v) {
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}
ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(load); });
Promise.resolve(ducter.get('items')).then(load);
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: the page is on your shelf with the four PRs. Tick one off when it lands and the count goes down.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2555 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs</div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<style>
  .k-row { display:flex; gap:12px; align-items:flex-start; cursor:pointer; }
  .k-row input { margin-top:4px; width:18px; height:18px; flex:0 0 auto; accent-color: var(--d-accent); }
  .k-row-main { flex:1; min-width:0; }
  .k-row.done .k-row-main { opacity:.5; text-decoration: line-through; }
  .k-row-sub { color: var(--d-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-top:2px; }
</style>
<script>
  var ITEMS = ducter.get("items") || [];
  var landed = {};

  function render() {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    ITEMS.forEach(function (it) {
      var li = document.createElement("li");
      var on = !!landed[it.id];
      var label = document.createElement("label");
      label.className = "k-row" + (on ? " done" : "");
      var box = document.createElement("input");
      box.type = "checkbox";
      box.checked = on;
      box … Element("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      var t = document.createElement("div");
      t.textContent = it.title;
      var s = document.createElement("div");
      s.className = "k-row-sub";
      s.textContent = on ? "Landed" : it.sub;
      main.appendChild(t);
      main.appendChild(s);
      label.appendChild(box);
      label.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(label);
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    var open = ITEMS.filter(function (it) { return !landed[it.id]; }).length;
    document.getElementById("count").textContent = String(open);
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = "of " + ITEMS.length + " PRs";
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(open + " waiting");
  }

  function save() {
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("landed", JSON.stringify(landed));
  }

  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
      if (typeof v === "string" && v) { try { landed = JSON.parse(v) || {}; } catch (e) { landed = {}; } }
      else if (v && typeof v === "object") { landed = v; }
      render();
    }, function () { render(); });
  } else {
    render();
  }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter #412 · Compaction cut","sub":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem #10287 · Retry on 502","sub":"CI is failing; needs a fix before review"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592 · Flaky compaction test","sub":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter #418 · Apps bar","sub":"Draft; not yet asked for review"}]}

```

### picture-question-zh

```

  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的奶黄色笔记本电脑插画，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色。

适合放在空状态里。屏幕空白正好表达"这里还没有内容"，画面柔和友好，不会让人觉得出错。用它时最好配一句文字，比如"还没有记录"加一个按钮。

另外有两点要注意：320×246 偏小，放大显示会糊，最好准备 2 倍或 3 倍的版本。奶黄色调要和你产品的主色搭得上，不然会显得突兀。
```

  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 mem 的 README 错别字改了，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task settings] 用户原话："顺手把 mem 的 README 错别字也改一下"

要做的：
- 找出仓库根目录 README（中英文版本如果有）里的错别字和明显笔误，逐处改正。
- 只改错字和笔误，不改措辞、结构、排版和技术内容；拿不准的地方不改，在报告里列出来。

完成标准：
- 只改 README 相关文件，在当前分支（ducter/mem）上提交。
- 报告里说明改了哪些地方（每处一行：原文 → 改后），以及拿不准没改的地方。

不要做的：
- 不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR。
- 不改其他文件。
```
